### CORRELATION GROUPS

- `2026_05_BUF_LA::SCORING_UP` (same, strong (qualitative)): game total over, either team total over, QB passing yards over, receiver yards/receptions over, anytime TD yes, total TDs over — These rise and fall together with game scoring and pace.
- `2026_05_BUF_LA::LA_SIDE` (same, strong (qualitative)): LA moneyline, LA spread, LA team total over, LA player props over — Team-side markets share the same game-script risk.
